- **postmortem-scribe**: Writes incident postmortems and captures learnings as guardrails. Use after incidents or major bug escapes. ([DeliberateCode/System2](https://github.com/DeliberateCode/System2/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/postmortem-scribe.md) · ★3)

- **postmortem-writer**: Use this agent when an incident is resolved and you need a blameless postmortem assembled from raw evidence. - **postmortem-writer**: Structured incident post-mortem writer — gathers facts, drafts a post-mortem, and optionally saves distilled incident memory. - **postmortem-writer**: Post-mortem document generation specialist. Generates structured Markdown documents with Summary, Impact, Timeline, Root Cause, and Action Items sections. - **postop-judge**: Blind post-op judge — the SECOND gate of the /scientific-method harness, pinned to Fable. Receives EXACTLY the pre-op context packet + the work subagent's judge-ready deliverable — never the driver's verdict or reasoning — and returns an independent ACCEPT / REJECT / INCONCLUSIVE plus a HIGH/MEDIUM/LOW confidence scored on the 4-question rubric. Its independence is the whole point: the second judge exists to catch what the driver glossed. ([Infernal-Crack-LED/nikke-sim](https://github.com/Infernal-Crack-LED/nikke-sim/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/postop-judge.md))
- **postrun**: Execution-outcome auditor in the L4 execute team. Reads outputs, logs, metrics, and execution artifacts after formal execution, evaluates what actually happened, classifies the outcome, and recommends anomaly routing when needed. ([Jiayi-Mao1027/RunBridge](https://github.com/Jiayi-Mao1027/RunBridge/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/postrun.md) · ★1)

- **poteto-code**: Code-writing delegate for poteto-mode playbook steps (feature, refactoring, bug fix, perf, hillclimb). - **poteto-explainer**: Synthesis and explanation delegate. Backs the `how` skill's explainer, the `why` skill's synthesizer, and `teach`. ([lightshifted/pstack-claude](https://github.com/lightshifted/pstack-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/poteto-explainer.md) · ★1)

([lightshifted/pstack-claude](https://github.com/lightshifted/pstack-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/poteto-explorer.md) · ★1)
- **poteto-explorer**: Read-only exploration, review, and auditing subagent (`EnableWriteTools: false`, `EnableMCPTools: false`, `EnableSubagentTools: false`). Use when spawning read-only explorers, reviewers, or judges from nested subagents where built-in `TypeName: "research"` is restricted, or whenever a strict read-only subagent is needed. - **poteto-fable**: Third-family writing delegate. Exists so `arena` and `architect` can fan out across genuinely different models instead of the same model N times. ([lightshifted/pstack-claude](https://github.com/lightshifted/pstack-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/poteto-fable.md) · ★1)
- **poteto-judge**: Highest-judgment delegate for poteto-mode. Use when a wrong call is expensive to unwind; route routine code changes to `poteto-code`.
